- **arm-instructions-query**: 查询 ARM NEON/SVE/SME 指令集详细信息及 ACLE（Arm C Language Extensions）内联函数。 当用户进行 ARM C 语言 intrinsic 编程或 Rust `std::arch::aarch64` 向量化编程时触发， 包括：使用 svadd、vaddq、__dmb 等 intrinsic；使用 Rust 的 vld1q_f32、vaddq_f32、 svcntw 等 std::arch::aarch64 内联函数；查指令详情、确认某架构有没有某指令、 NEON/SVE/SME 指令对比、指令功能分析；查询 intrinsic 原型、类型系统、特性检测宏、头文件依赖； NEON↔SVE 转换、SME 流模式、内存预取、对齐规则等 ARM 向量化编程问题。 Rust 侧：ACLE intrinsic 名与 std::arch::aarch64 的 Rust 内联函数同名（已稳定子集）， 结果里同时给出对应的 Rust 写法与 target_feature 要求。 ([openeuler-mirror/KPBot](https://github.com/openeuler-mirror/KPBot/tree/HEAD/Plugins/code-optimizer/opencode/arm-instructions-query) · 2 copies)

- **arm-lanes**: Internal dockwright trigger that starts the manager's lane monitors. ([taburetka123/dockwright](https://github.com/taburetka123/dockwright/tree/HEAD/deploy/plugin/plugins/dockwright/skills/arm-lanes) · ★1)
- **arm-level-copy-number-calling**: Use when you have a normalized, filtered binned scATAC-seq accessibility matrix and need per-cell chromosome arm-level gains/losses (e.g., to separate malignant from nonmalignant cells or define genetic subclones). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/arm-level-copy-number-calling))
- **arm-local-monitoring**: Watch an A2MC ensemble running on a WORKSTATION, with no scheduler — the local counterpart of arm-hpc-monitoring. Arms a Claude `Monitor` on the dispatcher log an `A2MC_EXEC_MODE=local` submission writes, checks liveness from the process table and the filesystem rather than from `squeue`/`sacct`, and says which of the HPC three-layer watcher contract applies off a scheduler and which does not. Use after submitting locally, when resuming a session with local runs in flight, or when someone asks how to monitor a run on their laptop. ([jingtao-lbl/A2MC](https://github.com/jingtao-lbl/A2MC/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arm-local-monitoring) · ★1)
- **arm-mcp-mcp**: Provides AI assistants with specialized tools for Arm architecture development, migration, optimization, and profiling. Includes knowledge base search, code migration analysis, container architecture inspection, Arm Performix workload profiling, and assembly performance analysis. ([Friz-zy/ai-capability-registry](https://github.com/Friz-zy/ai-capability-registry/tree/HEAD/mcp/servers/arm-mcp) · ★12)

- **arm-migration-agent**: Arm Cloud Migration Assistant accelerates moving x86 workloads to Arm infrastructure. It scans the repository for architecture assumptions, portability issues, container base image and dependency incompatibilities, and recommends Arm-optimized changes. It can drive multi-arch container builds, validate performance, and guide optimization, enabling smooth cross-platform deployment directly inside GitHub. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-arm-migration))
- **arm-motion-verify**: Use when verifying that a commanded joint on the Emre Kalem arm actually MOVED — camera-verified motion, control frames, the geometry gate and its limits. Trigger on "did the joint move", "verify the gripper/wrist", "prove it moved", any calibration-log motion claim, or before recording a joint as controllable. ([Mikecranesync/emre-kalem-robot-arm](https://github.com/Mikecranesync/emre-kalem-robot-arm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arm-motion-verify))
- **arm-mte-programming**: Use when writing or reviewing memory allocators, garbage collectors, or pointer-tagging code for Armv9/Android, or when debugging hardware tag faults. Covers ARM Memory Tagging Extension modes (SYNC/ASYNC/ASYMM), 16-byte granules, 4-bit tags, IRG/LDG/STG instructions, top-byte-ignore, and enabling MTE with prctl.
